			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-1a5ba12 elementor-invisible elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="1a5ba12" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-settings="{&quot;_animation&quot;:&quot;fadeIn&quot;}" data-widget_type="image.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
															<img loading="lazy" decoding="async" width="621" height="750" src="https://corporatednaconsulting.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/DNA_2326-rheaBook.jpg" class="attachment-large size-large wp-image-4418" alt="" srcset="https://corporatednaconsulting.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/DNA_2326-rheaBook.jpg 621w, https://corporatednaconsulting.com/wp-content/uploads/2021/09/DNA_2326-rheaBook-248x300.jpg 248w" sizes="(max-width: 621px) 100vw, 621px" />															</div>

				<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-50 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-1a4ee00" data-id="1a4ee00" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-0452f43 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="0452f43" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h2 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">Rhea Leckie</h2>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-8fc561f elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="8fc561f" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h5 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">Program Director and CEO of CorporateDNA</h5>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-d3af04f elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="d3af04f" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Rhea Leckie</span><span style="font-weight: 400;">, 47, i</span><span style="font-weight: 400;">s the Founder-CEO of CorporateDNA Consulting, with its European HQ in London and its APAC HQ in Singapore. An international thought leader in leadership, culture, inclusion and high performing teams, she acts as a trusted advisor with an ivy league clientele (Goldman Sachs, Vodafone, Heineken, Coca Cola and others). CorporateDNA was a national semi-finalist for the HSBC UK, 2009 business  awards. In 2008 Rhea was one of six finalists for the Women of the Future Awards, patroned by Cherie Blair for  being a successful entrepreneur in corporate Britain. In 2015 Rhea was a finalist for the British Indian Trade Industries  Awards under the Creative Entrepreneur category. She is also a published author for her  bestseller book – Leadership, It&#8217;s in your DNA (Bloomsbury, 2012). </span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Born in London, and having spent formative years in India, Rhea has worked and lived in over 20 countries through her career. Rhea</span><span style="font-weight: 400;"> carries diversity i</span><span style="font-weight: 400;">n her own DNA, celebrating &#8220;difference and being different&#8221; across her cross-cultural experiences in the UK, the US, Europe, the Middle East, East Asia, Africa, Japan, and India. While at PricewaterhouseCoopers UK, she was appointed one of their youngest directors at 31. Rhea has a consistent track record of working with management boards for over 20 years, including Chairmen, CEOs, HRDs and high performing teams. Rhea is best known for her authenticity and impact at all levels.</span></p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-59d1789 elementor-widget elementor-widget-toggle" data-id="59d1789" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="toggle.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
							<div class="elementor-toggle">
							<div class="elementor-toggle-item">
					<div id="elementor-tab-title-9411" class="elementor-tab-title" data-tab="1" role="button" aria-controls="elementor-tab-content-9411" aria-expanded="false">
												<span class="elementor-toggle-icon elementor-toggle-icon-left" aria-hidden="true">
															<span class="elementor-toggle-icon-closed"><i class="fas fa-caret-right"></i></span>
								<span class="elementor-toggle-icon-opened"><i class="elementor-toggle-icon-opened fas fa-caret-up"></i></span>
													</span>
												<a class="elementor-toggle-title" tabindex="0">Read more / Full Bio</a>
					</div>

					<div id="elementor-tab-content-9411" class="elementor-tab-content elementor-clearfix" data-tab="1" role="region" aria-labelledby="elementor-tab-title-9411"><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">An international thought leader and trusted advisor at the highest levels, Rhea Leckie, 47, is the Founder-CEO of Corporate DNA Consulting, with its European headquarters in London and its APAC offices in Singapore.</span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">With a core team of 62 coaches and facilitators across 16 nationalities, Corporate DNA was a national semi-finalist for the HSBC UK, 2009 Business Awards. In 2008, Rhea was one of six finalists for the Women of the Future Awards, with Cherie Blair’s patronage for being a successful entrepreneur in corporate Britain. In 2015, Rhea was a finalist for the British Indian Trade Industries Awards under the Creative Entrepreneur category.</span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">The Corporate DNA offering specialises in Culture Transformation, Leadership development, Talent development, I&amp;D, and Executive coaching. Working deeply with the top 5 of the FTSE 100 clients, Rhea is best known for her authenticity and impact at all levels. Clients include Heineken, Unilever, Goldman Sachs, Morgan Stanley, Aviva, Coca Cola, and Microsoft.</span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Born in London, and having spent formative years in India, Rhea has worked and lived in 28 countries. Rhea carries diversity in her own DNA – celebrating &#8220;difference and being different&#8221; across her cross-cultural experiences in the UK, the US, Europe, the Middle East, East Asia, Africa, Japan, and India.</span><span style="font-weight: 400;"> </span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Previously, while at PricewaterhouseCoopers UK, she was appointed one of their youngest directors at 31. Rhea has a consistent track record of working with management boards for over 20 years, including Chairmen, CEOs, HRDs and High Performing Teams.</span><span style="font-weight: 400;"> </span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Rhea is the </span><b>author of Leadership. It&#8217;s in your DNA published by Bloomsbury</b><span style="font-weight: 400;"> and released in November, 2012 where she reveals the 10 shared leadership ingredients innate in everyone from from Chairmen, CEOs and Ministers to maestros in sports, music, medicine, arts to everyday plumbers and chefs. In conversation with Sir George Martin, Producer of the legendary BEATLES, she convinces us through inspiring stories and insights her core belief: &#8220;You can do anything if you really want to.&#8221; She uses the 10 ingredients to structure her keynotes and coaching lessons with great success.</span><span style="font-weight: 400;"> </span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">A regular </span><b>commentator, author and speaker</b><span style="font-weight: 400;"> on talent and succession management, leadership, entrepreneurship and women in boards, Rhea has featured in the Wall Street Journal, Management Today, HR Director, The Economist, Telegraph and on the global Advisory Committee for Talent for the Conference Board.</span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Rhea is an </span><b>inspirational speaker</b><span style="font-weight: 400;"> for the UK and emerging countries. In 2013 Rhea was a distinguished speaker at the Chicago Business School for experienced VPs/Directors while in In 2011, Rhea lectured at the London School of Economics to Management students and in 2005 to the Gurukul Scholars in London Business School. In 2008 Rhea was invited by the Government of Malaysia&#8217; s Ministry of Women to share her &#8220;secrets of success&#8221; in the corporate world on a platform with their deputy prime minister and 1200 women. Involved in several voluntary and inspirational causes, Rhea was listed as one of 100 inspirational women by the ASHA foundation and featured by the International Women of Excellence in 2005. In 2009, Rhea was invited by the Ministry of Defence, UK to inspire women in London to advance their careers. In 2009, she was part of Cherie Blair&#8217;s delegation to tap into the entrepreneurial stories of slum dwellers in Mumbai.</span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Rhea has an MBA, is a neuroscience practitioner and a qualified accredited executive coach with a number of management and leadership certifications from INSEAD, Cranfield and Ashridge. Rhea speaks four languages, considers Italy her home and is married to Philip Leckie, who she shares her love for sports and travel with. Rhea has been living in Singapore since 2013.</span></p></div>

					const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
					const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
						entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
							if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
								let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
								if( lazyloadBackground ) {
									lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
								}
								lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
							}
						});
					}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
					lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
						lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
					} );
				};
				const events = [
					'DOMContentLoaded',
					'elementor/lazyload/observe',
				];
				events.forEach( ( event ) => {
					document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
